# Literature held locally — acquisition manifest (`lit/`)

Generated 2026-11-20 from the fetch ledgers (generator and ledgers were session scratch; the complete per-file map is `lit/SOURCES.tsv`, the checksums `lit/SHA256SUMS`).
Scope: every URL written in `pim/docs/*.md` (686 distinct strings), every ICOT TR/TM number cited there (327), the complete FGCS'84/'88/'92/'94 sets from the AIST-hosted ICOT archive, and the other document series of that archive that the notes cite by path. All paths below are relative to `pim/docs/nonlocal/` unless they start with `pim/`.

Fetched with plain `curl` and a generic user agent. A file counts as held only if it passed a content check (`%PDF`/`%!` magic and an `%%EOF` trailer for PDFs; HTML titles screened for error, challenge and parked-domain pages). Nothing downloaded was executed. Software archives and the IFS manuals are out of scope here (the `ifs/` acquisition holds them).

## Summary

- Files under `lit/`: **3156**, **2182726482 bytes** (2.18 GB). No file exceeds 95 MB (largest: journals/fstj/Fujitsu_Scientific_and_Technical_Journal_V26_N04_199102.pdf).
- Cited items by status (sections 1–3; one row per item): ALREADY HELD 12, FAILED [BASE-PATH] 4, FAILED [BLOCKED] 8, FAILED [DEAD] 13, FAILED [NOT-IN-ARCHIVE] 29, FAILED [TOO-BIG] 1, FULL TEXT 566, RECORD-ONLY 51
- FAILED by reason: BASE-PATH 4, BLOCKED 8, DEAD 13, NOT-IN-ARCHIVE 29, TOO-BIG 1. DEAD = the URL no longer serves the document (404, dead host, parked domain) and no usable Wayback capture exists; BLOCKED = the host refuses automated clients (403/429/401, bot challenge); NOT-IN-ARCHIVE = TR/TM number with no PDF in the archive; TOO-BIG = over the 95 MB per-file limit; BASE-PATH = a directory prefix cited as a base, entry page held.
- Cited URL strings that are search/API queries or placeholders, not documents: 221 (section 6). Software/archive URLs left to the `ifs/` acquisition: 17 (section 7).

- Reconciliation of the 686 cited URL strings: 260 IPSJ record/file URLs (= 130 records), 19 CiNii record URLs, 221 queries/placeholders, 17 software/archive URLs, 27 archive.org item URLs, 3 AIST TR PDF URLs (section 2), 1 AIST FGCS PDF URL (section 3), 2 already-held bitsavers URLs, 4 brace-list citations (expanded to 42 members), 132 other pages/documents (of which 10 J-STAGE URLs = 5 articles).

## 1. Items cited by URL

### 1.1 IPSJ digital library (one row per record; the landing URL `records/<id>` and the file URL are merged)

Every cited record has an open PDF. For each record the repository's JSON record (`lit/records/ipsj/<id>.json`, from `https://ipsj.ixsq.nii.ac.jp/api/records/<id>`) is held beside the PDF; the HTML landing page (the cited `records/<id>` URL, `lit/records/ipsj/<id>.html`) is held for 130 of the 130 records (the server took about 25 s per landing page; the rest were not fetched before the session ended, and the JSON carries the same bibliographic data). Citation text here is the record's own title and bibliographic line.

`lit/fgcs/1994/ps/knowledge-info.ps` is byte-identical to `helic2.ps` (same SHA-256): the two Wayback captures (1997-07-05/06) carry the same content under both names, so only one of those two papers is actually held; which one was not determined here.

## 2. ICOT Technical Reports and Memoranda cited by number

Source: `https://www.airc.aist.go.jp/aitec-icot/ICOT/Museum/TRTM/{TR,TM}/{TR,TM}pdf/`; the archive's own lists are `lit/trtm/TRlist.html` and `lit/trtm/TMlist.html`. Rights holder for all rows: ICOT / AITEC (see Rights). Numbers were extracted from the notes with the pattern `TR|TM[- ]?NNNN` (case-insensitive, zero-padded).

## 3. FGCS proceedings and reports (AIST archive `Museum/FGCS/`)

FGCS papers cited by author and title only have no row of their own; they resolve through the saved set index pages (`lit/fgcs/<year>/<set>/<set>.html`), which give title, authors and file name. 
The index pages list 929 per-paper files; HEAD requests totalled 743.5 MB before download (under the 1.5 GB threshold), so the complete sets were fetched: **968 files, 751436090 bytes** held. Per-file rows are in `lit/SOURCES.tsv`. `&` in server names (`R&D…`) is written `and` locally. The FGCS'92 proceedings are also held as the two bitsavers volume scans (`pim/docs/FGCS92_Volume_1.pdf`, `pim/docs/FGCS92_Volume_2.pdf`, ALREADY HELD).

| Set | Files | Bytes |
|---|---|---|

## 4. Other document series of the ICOT archive cited by path (literature_gaps.md Appendix D 1.7)

Mirrored by following links from the series index pages, restricted to the listed directories. Per-file rows are in `lit/SOURCES.tsv` (prefix `web/www.airc.aist.go.jp/`).

Ten files of `Museum/MACHINE/` (`pim-m-E.html` and nine `pim-m/pim-m-*.jpg` photos) were found byte-identical to files already held in `pim-m/` and `pim-m/photos/` and are not duplicated under `lit/` (ALREADY HELD, section 8).

Not mirrored, by decision (size or scope), and therefore still external: `Museum/Minutes/` (steering-committee papers, 7,764 files per the notes), `Museum/FUNDING/` (2,268), `Museum/FGCS92Demo/` (1,148; only the two cited pages `E/doc011.html`, `E/doc014.html` are held), `Museum/SOFTWARE/` (866), `Museum/VIDEO/` (video), `Museum/CONTEST/`, `Museum/ICRA/`, `Museum/IExchange/`, `Museum/InvitedResearcher/`, `Museum/PERSONS/`, `Museum/IFS/` and `ifs/` (the `ifs/` acquisition), and the AITEC half of the Ueda Lab mirror (`AITEC/Report`, `Ronbun`, `Seminar`).

## Rights

No licence has been inferred. Each group below gives the statement the source itself displays, quoted from the copy held locally, so that redistribution can be decided per group. "No statement" means none was found on the pages fetched, not that the material is free. Holding these files in a private working tree and publishing them are different acts; only groups A (conditionally), and single items in F, carry any explicit permission.

### A. ICOT / AITEC archive, hosted by AIST (`lit/trtm/`, `lit/fgcs/`, `lit/web/www.airc.aist.go.jp/`, and the old-host copies under `lit/web/www.icot.or.jp/`, `lit/web/www.jipdec.or.jp/`, `lit/web/ftp.icot.or.jp/`, `lit/fgcs/1994/ps/`)

- Terms page: https://www.airc.aist.go.jp/aitec-icot/ICOT/using-FGCSDA-E.html (local `lit/web/www.airc.aist.go.jp/using-FGCSDA-E.html`): "AITEC holds copy right of all the contents of the FGCS Digital Archive as well as copy right of ICOT Free Software. (AITEC took over the copy right from ICOT.) AITEC permits free use of the contents of the FGCS Digital Archive for academic purposes without changing any parts of the contents. [...] To use the contents for commercial and other purposes, permission of AITEC is required."
- The archive entry page (https://www.airc.aist.go.jp/aitec-icot/, local `lit/web/www.airc.aist.go.jp/_root_index.html`) is the 2005 DVD front page and ends "copyright 2005 JIPDEC".
- Explicit terms: yes, **free use for academic purposes, unmodified**. Redistribution is not named separately; commercial or other use needs permission from AITEC, and the contact given on the page is an `icot.or.jp` mailbox (that domain did not resolve for `ftp.icot.or.jp` during this acquisition). The archive pages do not say whether the proceedings papers are also subject to their original publishers' rights; that was not investigated here.
- The ICOT Free Software terms (`lit/web/www.airc.aist.go.jp/using-IFS-E.html`: "Persons wanting to use the Program ("Users") may freely do so and may also freely modify and copy the Program") cover the software, not these documents.

### B. IPSJ digital library (`lit/ipsj/`, `lit/records/ipsj/`)

- Per-file licence field in the repository record, where present (49 of 130 records): "Copyright (c) <year> by the Information Processing Society of Japan". The other 81 records carry no licence text (`licensetype: license_free` with an empty statement, which is the repository's free-text licence slot, not a free licence).
- IPSJ copyright regulations, https://www.ipsj.or.jp/copyright/ronbun/copyright.html (local `lit/web/_rights/ipsj_copyright.html`), example usage notice: "本著作物の著作権は情報処理学会に帰属します。本著作物は著作権者である情報処理学会の許可のもとに掲載するものです。ご利用に当たっては「著作権法」ならびに「情報処理学会倫理綱領」に従うことをお願いいたします。" ("The copyright of this material is retained by the Information Processing Society of Japan ...").
- Explicit redistribution terms: **none**. The PDFs are open to read; copyright is IPSJ's (for some SIG reports it reverts to the authors under the same regulations).

### C. J-STAGE journals (`lit/journals/jstage/`, landing pages under `lit/web/www.jstage.jst.go.jp/`)

- Article pages show "© 1989 人工知能学会" (JSAI) and the equivalent per year; the SICE article is the Society of Instrument and Control Engineers'.
- J-STAGE terms, https://www.jstage.jst.go.jp/static/pages/TermsAndPolicies/ForIndividuals/-char/en (local `lit/web/_rights/jstage_terms_en.html`), Article 2: "Copyrights and other rights regarding the Registered Data published on the Service belong to the Publishing Organizations or their authors [...] the approval of the copyright holders is required for reprinting, duplication, public transmission, translation, commercial use or making of derivative information".
- Explicit redistribution terms: **none; approval required**.

### D. Vendor technical reviews (`lit/journals/mitsubishi_giho/`, `lit/journals/fstj/`, `lit/web/www.hitachihyoron.com/`, index pages under `lit/web/www.mitsubishielectric.co.jp/`, `lit/web/www.oki.com/`, `lit/web/www.global.toshiba/`)

- Mitsubishi Denki Giho: whole-issue PDFs from the publisher's own back-number site; page footer "© Mitsubishi Electric Corporation" (local `lit/web/www.mitsubishielectric.co.jp/giho/index.html`). No reuse statement on the pages fetched.
- Hitachi Hyoron: footer "© Hitachi, Ltd. 1994. All rights reserved." with a link to サイトの利用条件 (site terms; that page was not found at the guessed URL and is not held) (local `lit/web/_rights/hitachihyoron_top.html`).
- FUJITSU Scientific & Technical Journal: whole-issue scans from bitsavers.org, not from Fujitsu. bitsavers displays no terms (its directory pages are bare indexes, and the site answered HTTP 403 to this client on the day, so the copies here come from the earlier session's downloads). The journal is Fujitsu's publication; the scans carry no separate licence. Fujitsu's own archive pages answer HTTP 429 (bot checkpoint) and could not be read.
- Oki and Toshiba: index pages only, no articles.
- Explicit redistribution terms: **none** in this group.

### E. Museum and institutional pages (`lit/web/museum.ipsj.or.jp/`, `lit/web/sts.kahaku.go.jp/`, `lit/web/www.kcg.ac.jp/`, `lit/web/archive.computerhistory.org/`, `lit/web/ithistory.org/`, `lit/web/www.tus.ac.jp/`, `lit/web/www2.cs.arizona.edu/`, and the remaining single pages under `lit/web/`)

- IPSJ Computer Museum: every page footer "All Rights Reserved, Copyright (C) Information Processing Society of Japan" (local `lit/web/_rights/museum_ipsj_about_index.html`).
- Kahaku (National Museum of Nature and Science) site policy, https://sts.kahaku.go.jp/sitepolicy/index.php (local `lit/web/_rights/kahaku_sitepolicy.html`): "当サイトのコンテンツは、明記されているものを除き、すべて国立科学博物館に帰属します。当サイトの内容等を許可なく複製･可変および販売･出版･放送等に利用しないようにお願いします。" (content belongs to the museum; do not copy, alter, sell, publish or broadcast without permission).
- Computer History Museum oral-history PDF, KCG, IT History Society, TUS, the Arizona JTEC page, the news/forum/package-tracker pages: ordinary copyrighted web pages; no reuse statement was looked for beyond the page itself.
- Explicit redistribution terms: **none; Kahaku explicitly asks for permission**.

### F. archive.org and bitsavers scans (`lit/web/archive.org/`, `lit/records/archive.org/`, `lit/web/bitsavers.org/`, `lit/web/web.archive.org/`)

- `primetimefreewar0000unse` (Prime Time Freeware for AI, 1-1): item metadata `rights`: "Made publicly available at the request of the authors and rights holders on February 16, 2021", `licenseurl` http://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/ — **explicit: CC BY 4.0** (per the item record, local `lit/records/archive.org/primetimefreewar0000unse.json`).
- `DTIC_ADA151274`: a DTIC report in the `usgovernmentmirrors` / `government-documents` collections; the item states no rights.
- `TNM_Introduction_of_the_Fifth_Generation_Computer_...`: by its title an ICOT brochure, scanned in the Ted Nelson junk-mail collection; the item states no rights.
- The 21 lending-library books are not held (metadata only): they are `access-restricted-item` and were not downloaded.
- archive.org's terms page (https://archive.org/about/terms) is a JavaScript shell to curl; the saved copy (`lit/web/_rights/archive_org_terms.html`) contains no terms text.
- Wayback captures of old ICOT/KLIC pages (`lit/web/web.archive.org/`, `lit/web/www.icot.or.jp/`, `lit/fgcs/1994/ps/`) are ICOT/AITEC content: see group A. The KLIC tutorial (`tutorial.tex`) and "Inside KLIC" pages carry their authors' names and no licence on the pages fetched.
- bitsavers index pages: no statement.

### G. Catalogue and bibliographic records (`lit/records/cinii/`, `lit/web/ci.nii.ac.jp/`, `lit/web/ndlsearch.ndl.go.jp/`, `lit/web/link.springer.com/`, `lit/web/bibtex.github.io/`)

- Records only, no full text. CiNii and NDL metadata are published by NII and the National Diet Library; their terms pages were not obtained (CiNii's support page URL guessed here returned 404; CiNii record pages answer an AWS WAF challenge to curl, so the records were taken from the public OpenSearch API). The Springer page is the publisher's landing page for an article of "New Generation Computing"; its PDF URL returned an HTML page instead of a PDF to curl, so only the landing page is held.
- Explicit redistribution terms: **none obtained**.

### H. Mirrors of the ICOT Free Software area (`lit/web/www.cs.cmu.edu/`, `lit/web/www.uedalab.jp/`, `lit/web/www.softwarepreservation.org/`)

- CMU AI Repository, `areas/icot/0.html` (local `lit/web/www.cs.cmu.edu/afs/cs/project/ai-repository/ai/areas/icot/0.html`): "Copying: Copyright (c) 1991-93 ICOT. Use, copying, modification, and distribution permitted. See the copyright files in each package for details." — **explicit**, and it describes the IFS software packages listed on those index pages (which are not held here), not the index pages themselves.
- softwarepreservation.org: the Nishikawa et al. LPW'83 paper is posted there without a licence statement on the page fetched.

### Summary for the redistribution decision

| Group | Explicit terms shown | What they allow |
|---|---|---|
| A. ICOT/AITEC archive (TR/TM, FGCS, Museum series) | yes | free use for academic purposes, unmodified; other use needs AITEC's permission |
| B. IPSJ | copyright notice only | nothing beyond reading; IPSJ (or authors) hold copyright |
| C. J-STAGE (JSAI, SICE) | yes, restrictive | duplication and public transmission need the holder's approval |
| D. Vendor technical reviews | copyright notices only | nothing stated |
| E. Museum pages | Kahaku: restrictive; others: copyright notices | Kahaku asks for permission before copying |
| F. archive.org / bitsavers | one item CC BY 4.0; others none | Prime Time Freeware scan may be redistributed with attribution |
| G. Catalogue records | none obtained | — |
| H. IFS mirrors | yes (for the software) | "Use, copying, modification, and distribution permitted" (ICOT, per CMU index) |
